Strategy: if value is in SI range but NOT in US range, convert\n    # If value is in both ranges, use more sophisticated logic\n\n    in_us_range = us_min <= value <= us_max\n    in_si_range = si_min <= value <= si_max\n\n    if in_us_range and not in_si_range:\n        # Clearly in US range\n        return value\n\n    if in_si_range and not in_us_range:\n        # Clearly in SI range - convert\n        if callable(factor):\n            return factor(value)\n        else:\n            return value * factor\n\n    if not in_us_range and not in_si_range:\n        # Check if SI conversion is defined\n    if conv['si_range'] is None:\n        # No SI conversion, return as is\n        return value\n\n    si_min, si_max = conv['si_range']\n    factor = conv['si_to_us']\n\n    # Strategy: if value is in SI range but NOT in US range, convert\n    # If value is in both ranges, use more sophisticated logic\n\n    in_us_range = us_min <= value <= us_max\n    in_si_range = si_min <= value <= si_max\n\n    if in_us_range and not in_si_range:\n        # Clearly in US range\n        return value\n\n    if in_si_range and not in_us_range:\n        # Clearly in SI range - convert\n        if callable(factor):\n            return factor(value)\n        else:\n            return value * factor\n\n    if not in_us_range and not in_si_range:\n        #
